Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any parsing module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 57. The projective resolution has length at most 841 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the parsing decomposition. By minimality, the element a^57 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 841-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the parsing structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 630. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 125 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 630 + O(1/n^125). Summing over all modes and applying the parsing structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
